class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
const routes = {
  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,
  "/search": renderSearch,
};

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}
export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function useFetch(url) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ loading: true, data: null, error: null });
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(url)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data, error: null }); })
      .catch((error) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data: null, error }); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [url]);
  return state;
}

class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
const routes = {
  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,
  "/search": renderSearch,
};

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function useFetch(url) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ loading: true, data: null, error: null });
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(url)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data, error: null }); })
      .catch((error) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data: null, error }); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [url]);
  return state;
}
class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}
const routes = {
  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,
  "/search": renderSearch,
};

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}

export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}

export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
const routes = {
  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,
  "/search": renderSearch,
};

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function useFetch(url) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ loading: true, data: null, error: null });
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(url)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data, error: null }); })
      .catch((error) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data: null, error }); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [url]);
  return state;
}
export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
const routes = {
  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,
  "/search": renderSearch,
};

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}
class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }

    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function useFetch(url) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ loading: true, data: null, error: null });
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(url)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data, error: null }); })
      .catch((error) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data: null, error }); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [url]);
  return state;
}
export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

  };
}

class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
const routes = {
  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,
  "/search": renderSearch,
};

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}
export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function useFetch(url) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ loading: true, data: null, error: null });
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(url)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data, error: null }); })
      .catch((error) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data: null, error }); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [url]);
  return state;
}

class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}
class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
class RingBuffer {
  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);
    if (this.#items.length > this.#capacity) this.#items.shift();
  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }
  *[Symbol.iterator]() { yield* this.#items; }
}
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function useFetch(url) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ loading: true, data: null, error: null });
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(url)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data, error: null }); })
      .catch((error) => { if (!cancelled) setState({ loading: false, data: null, error }); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [url]);
  return state;
}
export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}
const routes = {
  "/": renderHome,
  "/users/:id": renderUser,
  "/search": renderSearch,
};

async function dispatch(path) {
  const handler = routes[path] ?? renderNotFound;
  try {
    return await handler(parseQuery(location.search));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`dispatch failed for ${path}:`, err);
    return renderError(err);
  }
}

export function debounce(fn, waitMs) {
  let handle = null;
  return (...args) => {
    if (handle !== null) clearTimeout(handle);
    handle = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), waitMs);
  };
}

  #items = [];
  #capacity;
  constructor(capacity) { this.#capacity = capacity; }
  push(x) {
    this.#items.push(x);

  }
  get length() { return this.#items.length; }

}
